# Agents · I · part 3

957 entries, incident-commander to instruction-critic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **incident-commander**: Leads production incident response, coordination, mitigation decisions, recovery validation, and post-incident improvement. Use during active incidents and for structured incident reviews. Not for routine monitoring (site-reliability) or normal bug triage (qa-engineer). Does not implement fixes, and does not spawn other agents — it directs the humans and specialist agents who do, via its caller. ([AlexanderVegazo26/agents](https://github.com/AlexanderVegazo26/agents/blob/HEAD/commandcode-suite/agents/incident-commander.md) · 4 copies)
- **incident-commander**: Orchestrates the war-room incident response model — dispatches specialist sub-agents, synthesises their findings, surfaces a typed-token confirmation card for any prod-touching action. Use when an incident is declared. ([Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates](https://github.com/Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness-devops/agents/incident-commander.md) · 2 copies)
- **incident-commander**: Coordinates the response to a live production incident — declares severity, assigns roles, keeps the timeline, drives mitigation, and runs status comms. Use PROACTIVELY the moment a customer-impacting incident is declared (debugger finds the root cause; you run the response and keep everyone coordinated). ([e7hdk/plug-in](https://github.com/e7hdk/plug-in/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/incident-commander.md))

Distinct from hotfix, which fixes the fire; incident-commander coordinates the response. - **index**: Antiphon 通过与本地 `antiphond` 守护进程通信的小型适配器来聆听智能体会话。每个适配器都是**故障即放行（fail-open）**的：如果 Antiphon 未安装或未运行，你的智能体的行为与插件不存在时完全一致。会话绝不会因守护进程而阻塞。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hans/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon 透過小巧的轉接器聆聽代理工作階段，這些轉接器與本機的 `antiphond` 常駐程式溝通。每個轉接器都是 **fail-open**：如果沒有安裝或沒有執行 Antiphon，你的代理的行為就跟外掛不存在時一模一樣。工作階段永遠不會因為常駐程式而被卡住。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hant/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon listens to agent sessions through small adapters that talk to the local `antiphond` daemon. Every adapter is **fail-open**: if Antiphon isn't installed or isn't running, your agent behaves exactly as if the plugin didn't exist. Nothing ever blocks a session on the daemon. ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/docs/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon слушает сессии агентов через небольшие адаптеры, которые общаются с локальным демоном `antiphond`. Каждый адаптер — **fail-open**: если Antiphon не установлен или не запущен, агент ведёт себя ровно так, как будто плагина не существует. Ничто и никогда не блокирует сессию из-за демона.
